them But before I go on really quickly I just wanted to I just want to say because I'm talking about Gun issues that I have been a previous gun owner I was married to a state trooper and a michigan National guard military policeman I'm very well educated with guns and I I am not against the Second amendment in any way I just don't want people to think that I'm this crazy person Screaming about guns that I know nothing about so you might think why am I talking about this National issue at a small City Council meeting but it's because it's local what we do or don't Do what the school board does or doesn't do what our police do and doesn't do matters if it happens Here it's going to get really local really fast and I worry because I wonder if we're doing enough And it's not just us but all of us from local councils to congress to the president and whatever Presidents we've had before this is a non -partisan rant no one with the power to help is doing enough And I'm scared and sad and I am furious that the next day after the mayor read his gun violence Proclamation that 19 children were slaughtered in texas there's been at least five more mass Shootings since then since we last sat here we have to fix this at every level but we're so Divided and so stuck on what the problem is there's a it's a mental health problem crowd And I'll tell you as a past therapist for troubled youth that it's absolutely a mental health problem I don't think anyone in their right mind goes in and shoots up a group of people especially children But you don't get to just say that it's it's not guns it's mental health and walk away you have to Address it we talk about putting police officers in school which I have supported but what about More therapists what about people who can actually help the students that are flagged by teachers Why aren't we making mental health services easier to get and easier to afford for everyone But instead of throwing money and resources at the problem we're throwing guns it's too easy for People with mental health issues to get weapons and we have to make that more difficult when I Was married to a state trooper he had a another trooper was killed by a drunk driver on the side Of the road by a car and that drunk driver was caught and brought back to the state police post And people were getting off duty and walking into this police post and they had top brass there Disarming the policemen as they walked in because they knew that even trained officers who aren't in Their right state of mind should not have easy access to weapons and thank god they did that Because I don't know if my husband at the time would have made the right decisions in that state Of mind this is a really long way of saying that we have to fix this I hate listening to these kids Talk about what is happening to them I know congress is finally trying but I'm furious that It took this long to act and I fear it won't be enough and I hope I'm wrong thank you thank you

Taxpayers interested in scheduling a taxpayer assistance meeting can call 248 -858 -0611 or they may visit www .oakgov .com slash treasurer. Some of the resources available are the michigan homeowners assistance fund which is a state grant That will cover up to 25 000 of delinquent taxes from 19 2019 and forward mortgage payments and Utility bills you must be for a private primary residence and make less than a hundred oh I'm sorry less than 150 percent ami and prove covet hardship in order to qualify There's a Financial Empowerment Center in the Treasurer's Office, which gives one -on -one financial coaching, and Lakeshore Legal Aid provides free legal services to people who Are low -income and seniors. Again, we are here to help, and strongly encourage taxpayers to contact the Treasurer's Office If they have delinquent taxes. If taxpayers are interested in scheduling taxpayer assistance meetings, they can call the number that I just mentioned. Thank you. That's all.

I had not done enough research into the experiences of other communities, the broader privacy Questions surrounding the license plate reader technology, or the ways this data could potentially Be accessed or used beyond our own police department. I should have. That was my responsibility as a Councilmember, and I apologize to the residents for not doing the due diligence that I should have done. Since then, I've spent considerably more time looking at these issues, and what I have learned Has changed my position. My concern is not primarily about how our own police Department intends to use this technology but what happens after the What happens is information once it exists. As everybody has stated there's Been troubling examples and allegations around the country involving the sharing And use of license plate reader data including its use to locate and track People who are not suspecting of committing a crime that can that should Concern us all and even if we have complete confidence in our own chief and officers which I do we cannot guarantee the judgment policies or ethics or future leadership of every other Outside agency that may have access to the information originating from our community We also cannot know what state or federal governments may require in the future That part is particularly troubling to me we can establish policies today about retention Access, and appropriate use. But if state or federal laws change, if we receive Orders requiring this data to be turned over, our ability as a city to say no may Be extremely limited. Future laws could require data to be retained longer or to Be used in ways that this council and our residents would never agree to. Once This type of surveillance infrastructure exists, we cannot guarantee that the Rules governing it will remain the rules we agreed to when we installed it. But But there is one other consideration for me That carries just as much weight. We are a representative government. The people sitting here at this table Were elected to represent the people who live here. And since this issue has come forward, I've heard from an extraordinary number of people On email, here tonight, in person, Asking us to end this contract. And this has not been a partisan issue. For those reasons, I Will be voting when the vote does come up to discontinue our contract with Flood. And once again to our residents, I apologize for not doing a level of research I should have done before my original vote. Thank you.
